import ru.iflex.timemvp.R
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.Accuracy
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.beatsPerSecondLength
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.defaultBeatsPerSecond
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui.SettingsContract.Event
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_lib.Icon
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_lib.Screen
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_lib.ToggleBox
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.Dimens
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.baseBackgroundColor
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.baseTextStyle
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.disabledTextColor
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.disabledTextFieldStyle
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.disabledTextStyle
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.smallShape
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.textFieldBackgroundColor

@Composable
fun SettingScreen(
    drawerState: DrawerState
) {
    // Подключение вьюмодели
    val viewModel = koinViewModel<SettingsViewModel>()

    // Состояние экрана
    val state = viewModel.viewState.value

    // События
    val setEvent: (Event) -> Unit = { viewModel.setEvent(event = it) }

    Screen(
        title = state.title,
        drawerState = drawerState
    ) {

        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background((MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseBackgroundColor)),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(Dimens.verticalPadding)
        ) {
            //region "Звук"
            ToggleBox(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(
                    start = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                    top = Dimens.largeVerticalPadding,
                    end = Dimens.horizontalPadding
                ),
                text = R.string.sound,
                isOn = state.isSoundMode,
                onToggleClick = {
                    setEvent(Event.SwitchSoundMode)
                }
            )
            //endregion

            //region "Вибрация"
            ToggleBox(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(
                    start = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                    top = Dimens.largeVerticalPadding,
                    end = Dimens.horizontalPadding
                ),
                text = R.string.vibration,
                isOn = state.isVibrationMode,
                onToggleClick = {
                    setEvent(Event.SwitchVibrationMode)
                }
            )
            //endregion

            //region "Cheer-Ups"
            ToggleBox(
                modifier = Modifier.padding(
                    start = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                    top = Dimens.largeVerticalPadding,
                    end = Dimens.horizontalPadding
                ),
                text = R.string.cheer_ups,
                isOn = state.isCheerUpsEnabled,
                onToggleClick = {
                    setEvent(Event.ToggleCheerUps)
                }
            )
            //endregion

            //region "Ударов в минуту"
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(
                        start = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                        top = Dimens.largeVerticalPadding,
                        end = Dimens.horizontalPadding
                    )
                    .fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Start,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
            ) {
                if (!(state.isSoundMode || state.isVibrationMode)) {
                    Icon(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .padding(end = Dimens.horizontalPadding),
                        painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.ic_lock),
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.disabledTextColor
                    )
                }
                BasicText(
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1F),
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.beats_per_second),
                    style = if (state.isSoundMode || state.isVibrationMode) {
                        baseTextStyle
                    } else {
                        disabledTextStyle
                    }
                )

                BasicTextField(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .background(
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.textFieldBackgroundColor,
                            shape = smallShape
                        )
                        .width(Dimens.toggleWidth)
                        .focusable()
                        .onFocusChanged { focusState ->
                            if (!focusState.isFocused) {
                                if (state.beatsPerSecond.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    setEvent(Event.SetBeatsPerSecond(state.beatsPerSecond.toInt()))
                                } else {
                                    setEvent(Event.SetBeatsPerSecond(defaultBeatsPerSecond))
                                }
                            }
                        },

                    value = state.beatsPerSecond,
                    onValueChange = {
                        if (it.isNotEmpty()) {
                            if (it.last().isDigit() && it.toInt() <= beatsPerSecondLength) {
                                setEvent(Event.ChangeBeatsPerSecond(it))
                            } else {
                                it.dropLast(1)
                            }
                        } else {
                            setEvent(Event.ChangeBeatsPerSecond(it))
                        }
                    },
                    textStyle = if (state.isSoundMode || state.isVibrationMode) {
                        baseTextStyle.copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                    } else {
                        disabledTextFieldStyle.copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
                           },
                    singleLine = true,
                    enabled = state.isSoundMode || state.isVibrationMode,
                    keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions.Default.copy(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                )
            }
            //endregion

            //region "Точность"
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(
                        start = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                        end = Dimens.horizontalPadding,
                        top = Dimens.largeVerticalPadding * 2
                    )
                    .fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Start,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
            ) {

                    Icon(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .padding(end = Dimens.horizontalPadding),
                        painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.ic_lock),
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.disabledTextColor
                    )

                BasicText(
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(

                    ),
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.accuracy),
                    style = disabledTextStyle//baseTextStyle
                )
            }
            Column(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .padding(horizontal = Dimens.horizontalPadding)
                    .background(
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.textFieldBackgroundColor,
                        shape = smallShape
                    )
                    .fillMaxWidth()
            ) {
                AccuracyItem(
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.deciseconds),
                    isSelected = state.accuracy == Accuracy.DECISECONDS,
                    onSelect = {
                        //setEvent(Event.SetAccuracy(Accuracy.DECISECONDS))
                    }
                )
                AccuracyItem(
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.centiseconds),
                    isSelected = state.accuracy == Accuracy.CENTISECONDS,
                    onSelect = {
                        //setEvent(Event.SetAccuracy(Accuracy.CENTISECONDS))
                    }
                )
                AccuracyItem(
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.milliseconds),
                    isSelected = state.accuracy == Accuracy.MILLISECONDS,
                    onSelect = {
                        //setEvent(Event.SetAccuracy(Accuracy.MILLISECONDS))
                    }
                )
            }
            //endregion
        }
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\settings\ui\SettingsViewModel.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui

import android.content.Intent
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import ru.iflex.timemvp.R
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.BaseViewModel
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.orientationMode
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.portraitMode
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui.SettingsContract.Effect
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui.SettingsContract.Event
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui.SettingsContract.State
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.settings.ui.use_case.SettingsUseCases
import ru.iflex.timemvp.use_case.ContextUseCase

class SettingsViewModel(
    private val context: ContextUseCase,
    private val settingsUseCases: SettingsUseCases,
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService
) : BaseViewModel<Event, State, Effect>() {
    override fun setInitialState() = State()

    init {
        with(context()) {
            Intent().also { intent ->
                intent.setAction(orientationMode)
                intent.putExtra(orientationMode, portraitMode)
                sendBroadcast(intent)
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            setState {
                copy(
                    title             = context.context.getString(R.string.settings_screen_title),
                    isSoundMode       = localStorage.getSoundOn(),
                    isVibrationMode   = localStorage.getVibrationOn(),
                    beatsPerSecond    = localStorage.getBeatsPerSecond().toString(),
                    accuracy          = localStorage.getAccuracy(),
                    isCheerUpsEnabled = localStorage.getCheerUpsEnabled()
                )
            }
        }
    }

    override fun handleEvents(event: Event) {
        when (event) {
            is Event.SwitchSoundMode -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    settingsUseCases.switchSoundMode(!viewState.value.isSoundMode)
                    setState { copy(isSoundMode = !viewState.value.isSoundMode) }
                }
            }

            is Event.SwitchVibrationMode -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    settingsUseCases.switchVibrationMode(!viewState.value.isVibrationMode)
                    setState { copy(isVibrationMode = !viewState.value.isVibrationMode) }
                }
            }

            is Event.SetBeatsPerSecond -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    settingsUseCases.setBeatsPerSecond(event.beats)
                }
            }

            is Event.ChangeBeatsPerSecond -> {
                viewModelScope.launch {
                    setState { copy(beatsPerSecond = event.beats) }
                }
            }

            is Event.SetAccuracy -> {
                settingsUseCases.setAccuracy(event.accuracy)
                setState { copy(accuracy = event.accuracy) }
            }

            is Event.ToggleCheerUps -> {
                val newValue = !viewState.value.isCheerUpsEnabled
                localStorage.putCheerUpsEnabled(newValue)
                setState { copy(isCheerUpsEnabled = newValue) }
            }
        }
    }
}

package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.PeriodFilter

data class TopDeviation(val errorMs: Double, val count: Int)

data class DeviationProfile(
    val medianMs: Double,
    val stdPct: Double
)

/** Одна попытка для отображения в дереве сессий */
data class AttemptBarData(
    val number  : Int,
    val errorMs : Double,   // знак: < 0 = early, > 0 = late, ≈ 0 = win
    val isWin   : Boolean   // true = попадание в окно точности квеста
)

data class SessionTreeData(
    val sessionId       : Int,
    val startTime       : Long,
    val endTime         : Long,
    val attempts        : Int,
    val wins            : Int,
    val isQuestComplete : Boolean        = false,
    val questLevelId    : Int            = 0,
    val questTaskId     : Int            = 0,
    val attemptBars     : List<AttemptBarData> = emptyList()
)

data class AnalyticsData(
    // ── Текущий период ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    val period         : PeriodFilter = PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME,
    val periodAttempts : Long         = 0L,
    val periodScm      : Double       = 0.0,

    // ── Сводка (всегда ALL_TIME) ──────────────────────────────────────
    val totalScm                : Double,
    val sessionsCount           : Long,
    val totalPlayTimeMs         : Long,
    val totalAttempts           : Long,
    val totalWins               : Long,
    val daysSinceStart          : Long,
    val startDateStr            : String,

    // ── Deviation Profile ─────────────────────────────────────────────
    val deviationAll   : DeviationProfile,
    val deviationEarly : DeviationProfile,
    val deviationLate  : DeviationProfile,

    // ── Range of Errors (97th percentile) ────────────────────────────
    val rangeMaxMs : Double,

    // ── True Three Hit Rate ───────────────────────────────────────────
    val trueThreeHitRatePct : Double,
    val attemptsPerWin      : Double,

    // ── Top Deviations ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    val topEarly : List<TopDeviation>,
    val topLate  : List<TopDeviation>,

    // ── Pace ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    val paceOfPlayPerMin     : Double,
    val paceOfLearning       : Double,
    val practiceDensityMinPerDay : Double,

    // ── Sparklines (последние 5 сессий) ──────────────────────────────
    val sparklinePaceOfLearning : List<Float> = emptyList(),
    val sparklineChanceOfWin    : List<Float> = emptyList(),

    // ── Дерево сессий ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    val sessions : List<SessionTreeData>
)

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\AnalyticsUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.PeriodFilter
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.SessionDatabaseModel
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TryDatabaseModel
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AnalyticsData
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AttemptBarData
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.DeviationProfile
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.SessionTreeData
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.TopDeviation
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.TimeZone
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.pow
import kotlin.math.roundToLong
import kotlin.math.sqrt

class AnalyticsUseCase(
    private val database: TimeDatabase,
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(
        period: PeriodFilter = PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME
    ): AnalyticsData {
        val statDao    = database.statisticDao()
        val tryDao     = database.tryDao()
        val sessionDao = database.sessionDao()
        val aimTime    = localStorage.getAimTime()

        // ── Базовые агрегаты (ALL_TIME для шапки Summary) ────────────────
        val totalScm        = statDao.getTotalSerenityBalance()
        val sessionsCount   = statDao.getTotalSessionsCount()
        val totalPlayTimeMs = statDao.getTotalTime()
        val totalAttempts   = statDao.getTotalAttemptsCount()
        val totalWins       = statDao.getTotalWinsCount(aimTime)
        val daysSinceStart  = statDao.getTotalDaysCount()
        val startTimestamp  = statDao.getTotalSessionsDate()

        val startDateStr = if (startTimestamp > 0L) {
            val sdf = SimpleDateFormat("dd MMM yyyy", Locale.ENGLISH)
            sdf.timeZone = TimeZone.getTimeZone("UTC")
            sdf.format(Date(startTimestamp))
        } else "—"

        // ── Попытки и сессии за выбранный период ─────────────────────────
        val fromTime = period.startTimeMs()
        val periodAttempts: List<TryDatabaseModel> = when (period) {
            PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME -> tryDao.getAllAttempts()
            PeriodFilter.SESSION  -> {
                val lastSession = runCatching { sessionDao.getCurrentSession() }.getOrNull()
                if (lastSession != null) tryDao.getAttemptsBySessionId(lastSession.id)
                else emptyList()
            }
            else -> tryDao.getAttemptsSince(fromTime)
        }

        val periodSessions: List<SessionDatabaseModel> = when (period) {
            PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME -> sessionDao.getAllSessions()
            PeriodFilter.SESSION  -> {
                val lastSession = runCatching { sessionDao.getCurrentSession() }.getOrNull()
                if (lastSession != null) listOf(lastSession) else emptyList()
            }
            else -> sessionDao.getSessionsSince(fromTime)
        }

        val periodScm = periodAttempts.mapNotNull { it.scm }.filter { it > 0 }.sum()

        // ── Ошибки периода ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        val errorsMs       = periodAttempts.map { (it.endTime - it.startTime - aimTime).toDouble() }
        val deviationAll   = computeProfile(errorsMs)
        val earlyErrorsMs  = errorsMs.filter { it < 0 }.map { abs(it) }
        val lateErrorsMs   = errorsMs.filter { it > 0 }
        val deviationEarly = computeProfile(earlyErrorsMs)
        val deviationLate  = computeProfile(lateErrorsMs)

        // ── Range of Errors (97th percentile) ────────────────────────────
        val absErrors  = errorsMs.map { abs(it) }.sorted()
        val rangeMaxMs = if (absErrors.isNotEmpty()) {
            val idx = (absErrors.size * 0.97).toInt().coerceIn(0, absErrors.size - 1)
            absErrors[idx]
        } else 0.0

        // ── True Three Hit Rate ───────────────────────────────────────────
        val trueThreeWins    = countTrueThree(periodAttempts, aimTime)
        val periodTotal      = periodAttempts.size.toLong()
        val trueThreeHitRate = if (periodTotal > 0)
            trueThreeWins.toDouble() / periodTotal * 100.0 else 0.0
        val attemptsPerWin   = if (trueThreeWins > 0)
            periodTotal.toDouble() / trueThreeWins else 0.0

        // ── Top Deviations ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        val topEarly = topDeviations(earlyErrorsMs, negated = true)
        val topLate  = topDeviations(lateErrorsMs,  negated = false)

        // ── Pace of Play (попыток/мин за период) ─────────────────────────
        val periodPlayMs  = periodSessions.sumOf { s ->
            if (s.endTime > s.startTime) s.endTime - s.startTime else 0L
        }
        val periodPlayMin = periodPlayMs / 60_000.0
        val paceOfPlay    = if (periodPlayMin > 0) periodTotal / periodPlayMin else 0.0

        // ── Pace of Learning (SCM / попытка за период) ───────────────────
        val paceOfLearning = if (periodTotal > 0) periodScm / periodTotal else 0.0

        // ── Practice Density (мин игры / день, всегда ALL_TIME) ──────────
        val practiceDensity = if (daysSinceStart > 0)
            (totalPlayTimeMs / 60_000.0) / daysSinceStart
        else 0.0

        // ── Допуск квеста (для подсветки попыток в допуске) ──────────────
        val toleranceMs = localStorage.getQuestCurrentToleranceMs().toDouble()

        // ── Sessions для дерева ───────────────────────────────────────────
        val sessionsList = periodSessions.map { session ->
            val sAttempts = tryDao.getAttemptsBySessionId(session.id)
            val wins      = countTrueThree(sAttempts, aimTime)

            // Бары попыток: green = true three OR в допуске квеста
            val bars = sAttempts.mapIndexed { idx, att ->
                val errMs       = (att.endTime - att.startTime - aimTime).toDouble()
                val isTrueThree = countTrueThree(listOf(att), aimTime) > 0
                val inTolerance = toleranceMs > 0 && abs(errMs) <= toleranceMs
                AttemptBarData(number = idx + 1, errorMs = errMs, isWin = isTrueThree || inTolerance)
            }

            SessionTreeData(
                sessionId       = session.id,
                startTime       = session.startTime,
                endTime         = session.endTime,
                attempts        = sAttempts.size,
                wins            = wins,
                isQuestComplete = session.isQuestComplete,
                questLevelId    = session.questLevelId,
                questTaskId     = session.questTaskId,
                attemptBars     = bars
            )
        }.sortedByDescending { it.startTime }

        // ── Sparklines (последние 5 сессий, хронологически) ──────────────
        val sparklineSessions = sessionsList.takeLast(5).sortedBy { it.startTime }
        val sparklinePace = sparklineSessions.map { s ->
            val sScm = s.attemptBars.sumOf { 0.0 }.let {
                // SCM уже в bars нет; берём из tryDao через sessionId
                tryDao.getAttemptsBySessionId(s.sessionId)
                    .mapNotNull { it.scm }.filter { it > 0 }.sum()
            }
            if (s.attempts == 0) 0f else (sScm / s.attempts).toFloat()
        }
        val sparklineWin = sparklineSessions.map { s ->
            if (s.attempts == 0) 0f else s.wins.toFloat() / s.attempts.toFloat()
        }

        return AnalyticsData(
            totalScm                 = totalScm,
            periodScm                = periodScm,
            sessionsCount            = sessionsCount,
            totalPlayTimeMs          = totalPlayTimeMs,
            totalAttempts            = totalAttempts,
            totalWins                = totalWins,
            daysSinceStart           = daysSinceStart,
            startDateStr             = startDateStr,
            deviationAll             = deviationAll,
            deviationEarly           = deviationEarly,
            deviationLate            = deviationLate,
            rangeMaxMs               = rangeMaxMs,
            trueThreeHitRatePct      = trueThreeHitRate,
            attemptsPerWin           = attemptsPerWin,
            topEarly                 = topEarly,
            topLate                  = topLate,
            paceOfPlayPerMin         = paceOfPlay,
            paceOfLearning           = paceOfLearning,
            practiceDensityMinPerDay     = practiceDensity,
            sparklinePaceOfLearning      = sparklinePace,
            sparklineChanceOfWin         = sparklineWin,
            sessions                     = sessionsList,
            periodAttempts           = periodTotal,
            period                   = period
        )
    }

    // ── Вспомогательные ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private fun countTrueThree(attempts: List<TryDatabaseModel>, aimTime: Int): Int {
        return attempts.count { attempt ->
            val decimals   = attempt.accuracy
            val factor     = 10.0.pow(decimals)
            val aimRounded = (aimTime / 1000.0 * factor).roundToLong()
            val durRounded = ((attempt.endTime - attempt.startTime) / 1000.0 * factor).roundToLong()
            aimRounded == durRounded
        }
    }

    private fun computeProfile(values: List<Double>): DeviationProfile {
        if (values.isEmpty()) return DeviationProfile(0.0, 0.0)
        val sorted = values.sorted()
        val median = if (sorted.size % 2 == 0)
            (sorted[sorted.size / 2 - 1] + sorted[sorted.size / 2]) / 2.0
        else sorted[sorted.size / 2]
        if (median == 0.0) return DeviationProfile(0.0, 0.0)
        val variance = values.map { (it - median).pow(2) }.average()
        val stdPct   = sqrt(variance) / abs(median) * 100.0
        return DeviationProfile(medianMs = median, stdPct = stdPct)
    }

    private fun topDeviations(valuesMs: List<Double>, negated: Boolean): List<TopDeviation> {
        if (valuesMs.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
        return valuesMs
            .map { (it / 10.0).roundToLong() * 10.0 }
            .groupingBy { it }
            .eachCount()
            .entries
            .sortedByDescending { it.value }
            .take(3)
            .map { (errMs, count) ->
                TopDeviation(errorMs = if (negated) -errMs else errMs, count = count)
            }
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\AttemptsPerWinsUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase

class AttemptsPerWinsUseCase(
    private val database: TimeDatabase,
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService,
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(): String {
        val totalAttempts = database.statisticDao().getTotalAttemptsCount()
        val totalWins = database.statisticDao().getTotalWinsCount(localStorage.getAimTime())
        var result = "0/$totalAttempts"
        if (totalWins > 0L) {
            result = (totalAttempts / totalWins).toString()
        }
        return result
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\AverageLessUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.Accuracy
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase
import kotlin.math.round

class AverageLessUseCase(
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService,
    private val database: TimeDatabase
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(): String {
        when (localStorage.getAccuracy()) {
            Accuracy.SECONDS -> {
                return round(database.statisticDao().getAverageLess(localStorage.getAimTime())).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.DECISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageLess(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 10.0) / 10.0).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.CENTISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageLess(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 100.0) / 100.0).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.MILLISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageLess(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 1000.0) / 1000.0).toString()
            }
        }
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\AverageMoreUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.Accuracy
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase
import kotlin.math.round

class AverageMoreUseCase (
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService,
    private val database: TimeDatabase
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(): String {
        when (localStorage.getAccuracy()) {
            Accuracy.SECONDS -> {
                return round(database.statisticDao().getAverageMore(localStorage.getAimTime())).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.DECISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageMore(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 10.0) / 10.0).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.CENTISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageMore(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 100.0) / 100.0).toString()
            }

            Accuracy.MILLISECONDS -> {
                return (round(database.statisticDao().getAverageMore(localStorage.getAimTime()) * 1000.0) / 1000.0).toString()
            }
        }
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\DailyStatisticsUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.formatDate
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui.DailyStatisticItemState

class DailyStatisticsUseCase(
    private val database: TimeDatabase,
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService,
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(): List<DailyStatisticItemState> {
        val list = database.statisticDao().getDailyStatistic(localStorage.getAimTime())
        val result = mutableListOf<DailyStatisticItemState>()
        var number = list.size
        list.map {
              val item =  DailyStatisticItemState(
                    number = number.toString(),
                    date = formatDate(it.date),
                    attempts = it.attempts.toString(),
                    wins = it.wins.toString()
                )

            number -= 1
            result.add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\domain\usecase\ExportToExcelUseCase.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.data.LocalStorageService
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.Accuracy
import ru.iflex.timemvp.database.TimeDatabase
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui.ExcelStatisticState

class ExportToExcelUseCase(
    private val database: TimeDatabase,
    private val localStorage: LocalStorageService,
) {
    suspend operator fun invoke(): List<ExcelStatisticState> {
        val list = database.statisticDao().exportToExcel(localStorage.getAimTime())
        val result = mutableListOf<ExcelStatisticState>()
        var attemptNumber = 1L

        for (i in list.indices) {
            val it = list[i]
            val item = ExcelStatisticState(
                date = it.date,
                time = it.time,
                sessionNumber = it.sessionNumber,
                attemptNumber = attemptNumber,
                attemptResult = it.attemptResult,
                errorTime = it.errorTime,
                accuracy = Accuracy.entries[it.accuracy].abbr,
                hand = it.hand
            )
            if (i < list.size - 1) {
                if (it.sessionNumber == list[i + 1].sessionNumber) {
                    attemptNumber += 1
                } else {
                    attemptNumber = 1L
                }
            }
            result.add(item)
        }
        return result
    }
}

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Row

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding

import androidx.compose.ui.text.style.TextAlign

import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.statisticColor
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.statisticTextStyle
import ru.iflex.timemvp.ui_theme.statisticVariantColor

@Composable
fun DailyStatisticItem(
    state: DailyStatisticItemState
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .padding(top = Dimens.verticalPadding / 2)
            .fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Text(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(5f),
            text = state.number,
            style = statisticTextStyle.copy(
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.statisticVariantColor,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        )
        Text(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(10f),
            text = state.date,
            style = statisticTextStyle.copy(
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.statisticColor,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        )
        Text(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(7f),
            text = state.attempts,
            style = statisticTextStyle.copy(
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.statisticVariantColor,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        )
        Text(
            modifier = Modifier.weight(5f),
            text = state.wins,
            style = statisticTextStyle.copy(
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.statisticVariantColor,
                textAlign = TextAlign.Center
            )
        )
    }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\ui\StatsContract.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui

import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.ViewEvent
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.ViewSideEffect
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.ViewState
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.PeriodFilter
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AnalyticsData

class StatsContract {
    data class State(
        val selectedPeriod: PeriodFilter = PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME,
        // Аналитика
        val analytics: AnalyticsData? = null,
        val analyticsLoading: Boolean = true,
        // Дерево: какие узлы раскрыты
        val expandedYears: Set<Int> = emptySet(),
        val expandedMonths: Set<String> = emptySet(),
        val expandedDays: Set<String> = emptySet(),
        val expandedSessions: Set<Int> = emptySet(),
        // ExportToExcel
        val displayExportToExcelResultDialog: Boolean = false,
        val exportToExcelResultDialogText: String = "",
        val displayAppSpinner: Boolean = false,
    ) : ViewState

    sealed class Event : ViewEvent {
        data object ExportToExel : Event()
        data class SelectPeriod(val period: PeriodFilter) : Event()
        data class ToggleYear(val year: Int) : Event()
        data class ToggleMonth(val key: String) : Event()
        data class ToggleDay(val key: String) : Event()
        data class ToggleSession(val sessionId: Int) : Event()

        sealed class Dialog : Event() {
            data class Display(val text: String) : Dialog()
            data object Close : Dialog()
        }

        sealed class AppSpinner : Event() {
            data object Display : AppSpinner()
            data object Close : AppSpinner()
        }
    }

    sealed class Effect : ViewSideEffect
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Типографика: Inter с letter-spacing как Helvetica Now Display
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

private val interProvider = GoogleFont.Provider(
    providerAuthority = "com.google.android.gms.fonts",
    providerPackage   = "com.google.android.gms",
    certificates      = R.array.com_google_android_gms_fonts_certs
)

private val InterFamily = FontFamily(
    Font(GoogleFont("Inter"), interProvider, FontWeight.Normal),
    Font(GoogleFont("Inter"), interProvider, FontWeight.Medium),
    Font(GoogleFont("Inter"), interProvider, FontWeight.SemiBold),
    Font(GoogleFont("Inter"), interProvider, FontWeight.Bold),
)

/** Большие цифры-метрики: tight letter-spacing (-0.02em) */
private fun metricStyle(
    size: androidx.compose.ui.unit.TextUnit,
    weight: FontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
    color: Color = Color.Unspecified
) = TextStyle(
    fontFamily    = InterFamily,
    fontSize      = size,
    fontWeight    = weight,
    letterSpacing = (-0.02).em,
    color         = color
)

/** Подписи и лейблы: wide letter-spacing (+0.08em) */
private fun labelStyle(
    size: androidx.compose.ui.unit.TextUnit = 11.sp,
    weight: FontWeight = FontWeight.Normal,
    color: Color = Color.Unspecified
) = TextStyle(
    fontFamily    = InterFamily,
    fontSize      = size,
    fontWeight    = weight,
    letterSpacing = 0.08.em,
    color         = color
)

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Цветовые константы зон (Activation Spectrum)
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

private val cardBg     = Color(0xFF1A1A1A)
private val dividerClr = Color(0xFF2A2A2A)
private val subtleText = 0.45f   // alpha для неактивного текста

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  StatScreen — точка входа
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
fun StatScreen(drawerState: DrawerState) {
    val viewModel  = koinViewModel<StatsViewModel>()
    val state      = viewModel.viewState.value
    val setEvent: (Event) -> Unit = { viewModel.setEvent(it) }

    Screen(
        title          = "Statistics",
        drawerState    = drawerState,
        actionIconId   = R.drawable.ic_export,
        onActionIconClick = { setEvent(Event.ExportToExel) }
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseBackgroundColor)
        ) {
            when {
                state.analyticsLoading -> CircularProgressIndicator(
                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                    color    = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.accentColor
                )

                state.analytics != null -> AnalyticsContent(
                    data             = state.analytics,
                    selectedPeriod   = state.selectedPeriod,
                    expandedYears    = state.expandedYears,
                    expandedMonths   = state.expandedMonths,
                    expandedDays     = state.expandedDays,
                    expandedSessions = state.expandedSessions,
                    onSelectPeriod   = { setEvent(Event.SelectPeriod(it)) },
                    onToggleYear     = { setEvent(Event.ToggleYear(it)) },
                    onToggleMonth    = { setEvent(Event.ToggleMonth(it)) },
                    onToggleDay      = { setEvent(Event.ToggleDay(it)) },
                    onToggleSession  = { setEvent(Event.ToggleSession(it)) }
                )

                else -> BasicText(
                    text     = "No data yet",
                    modifier = Modifier.align(Alignment.Center),
                    style    = labelStyle(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor.copy(alpha = subtleText))
                )
            }

            if (state.displayExportToExcelResultDialog) {
                InfoDialog(
                    message      = state.exportToExcelResultDialogText,
                    onCloseClick = { setEvent(Event.Dialog.Close) }
                )
            }
            if (state.displayAppSpinner) AppSpinner()
        }
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Основное содержимое
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun AnalyticsContent(
    data             : AnalyticsData,
    selectedPeriod   : PeriodFilter,
    expandedYears    : Set<Int>,
    expandedMonths   : Set<String>,
    expandedDays     : Set<String>,
    expandedSessions : Set<Int>,
    onSelectPeriod   : (PeriodFilter) -> Unit,
    onToggleYear     : (Int) -> Unit,
    onToggleMonth    : (String) -> Unit,
    onToggleDay      : (String) -> Unit,
    onToggleSession  : (Int) -> Unit
) {
    val tree = remember(data.sessions) { buildTree(data.sessions) }
    var helpText by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

    Box(Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
    LazyColumn(
        modifier            = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        contentPadding      = PaddingValues(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
    ) {
        // ── Шапка: Total SCM всегда ALL_TIME ────────────────────────────
        item { SummaryHeader(data, onScmHelp = {
            helpText = "Serenity Coin (SCM) — валюта спокойствия. Начисляется за точность и стабильность: учитывает не только попадание в цель, но и консистентность — связь текущей попытки с соседними. Чем спокойнее и стабильнее система, тем больше монет."
        }) }

        // ── Period Pill ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
        item { PeriodPill(selectedPeriod, onSelectPeriod) }

        // ── Activation Spectrum (Deviation Profile) ──────────────────────
        item { ActivationSpectrumCard(data, onHelp = {
            helpText = "Activation Spectrum показывает баланс между возбуждением (ранние нажатия) и торможением (поздние). Early — система активировалась слишком рано, Late — слишком поздно. Медиана = типичная ошибка, σ% = её разброс."
        }) }

        // ── Range of Errors ──────────────────────────────────────────────
        item {
            StatCard(title = "RANGE OF ERRORS", onHelp = {
                helpText = "Attempts Range: диапазон, в который попадает 97% результатов без случайных выбросов. Показывает реальную зону «чувства времени» без учёта случайных сбоев."
            }) {
                CardMetricRow(
                    label = "97th percentile",
                    value = formatMs(data.rangeMaxMs),
                    note  = "without outliers"
                )
            }
        }

        // ── True Three Hit Rate ──────────────────────────────────────────
        item {
            StatCard(title = "CHANCE OF WIN", onHelp = {
                helpText = "True Three Hit Rate — доля попыток, в которых вы попали точно в цель с нужной точностью. Показывает, как часто система достигает спокойного состояния за одну попытку."
            }) {
                CardMetricRow(
                    label      = "Hit rate",
                    value      = "%.1f%%".format(data.trueThreeHitRatePct),
                    valueColor = resultGreenColor
                )
                CardMetricRow(
                    label = "Attempts per win",
                    value = if (data.attemptsPerWin > 0) "%.1f".format(data.attemptsPerWin) else "—"
                )
                if (data.sparklineChanceOfWin.size >= 2) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    TrendSparkline(data.sparklineChanceOfWin, resultGreenColor.copy(alpha = 0.7f))
                }
            }
        }

        // ── Top Deviations ───────────────────────────────────────────────
        item { TopDeviationsCard(data, onHelp = {
            helpText = "Modes of Deviation: ваши наиболее типичные ошибки — значения, которые повторяются чаще всего. Early (красные) = система возбуждена, нажатие раньше цели. Late (синие) = торможение, нажатие позже цели."
        }) }

        // ── Pace ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
        item {
            val paceLabel = when (selectedPeriod) {
                PeriodFilter.SESSION  -> "per session"
                PeriodFilter.TODAY    -> "per day"
                PeriodFilter.MONTH    -> "per month"
                PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME -> "overall"
            }
            StatCard(title = "PACE", onHelp = {
                helpText = "Pace of Play — скорость попыток (попыток в минуту). Pace of Learning — прирост SCM на одну попытку: насколько быстро система обучается. Practice Density — сколько минут в день вы тренируетесь."
            }) {
                CardMetricRow(
                    label = "Pace of Play",
                    value = if (data.paceOfPlayPerMin > 0)
                        "%.1f att/min".format(data.paceOfPlayPerMin) else "—"
                )
                CardMetricRow(
                    label = "Pace of Learning $paceLabel",
                    value = "%.1f SCM/att".format(data.paceOfLearning)
                )
                if (data.sparklinePaceOfLearning.size >= 2) {
                    Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
                    TrendSparkline(
                        data.sparklinePaceOfLearning,
                        MaterialTheme.colorScheme.accentColor.copy(alpha = 0.65f)
                    )
                }
                CardMetricRow(
                    label = "Practice Density",
                    value = if (data.practiceDensityMinPerDay > 0)
                        "%.1f min/day".format(data.practiceDensityMinPerDay) else "—"
                )
            }
        }

        // ── Sessions Tree ────────────────────────────────────────────────
        item {
            val baseColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
            BasicText(
                text  = "SESSIONS",
                style = labelStyle(size = 10.sp, weight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    color = baseColor.copy(alpha = subtleText))
            )
        }

        tree.forEach { yearNode ->
            item {
                TreeYearRow(
                    year      = yearNode.year,
                    attempts  = yearNode.totalAttempts,
                    wins      = yearNode.totalWins,
                    expanded  = yearNode.year in expandedYears,
                    onClick   = { onToggleYear(yearNode.year) }
                )
            }
            if (yearNode.year in expandedYears) {
                yearNode.months.forEach { monthNode ->
                    val monthKey = "${yearNode.year}-${monthNode.month}"
                    item {
                        TreeMonthRow(
                            label    = monthNode.label,
                            attempts = monthNode.totalAttempts,
                            wins     = monthNode.totalWins,
                            expanded = monthKey in expandedMonths,
                            onClick  = { onToggleMonth(monthKey) }
                        )
                    }
                    if (monthKey in expandedMonths) {
                        monthNode.days.forEach { dayNode ->
                            val dayKey = "${yearNode.year}-${monthNode.month}-${dayNode.day}"
                            item {
                                TreeDayRow(
                                    label    = dayNode.label,
                                    attempts = dayNode.totalAttempts,
                                    wins     = dayNode.totalWins,
                                    expanded = dayKey in expandedDays,
                                    onClick  = { onToggleDay(dayKey) }
                                )
                            }
                            if (dayKey in expandedDays) {
                                items(dayNode.sessions) { session ->
                                    TreeSessionRow(
                                        session  = session,
                                        expanded = session.sessionId in expandedSessions,
                                        onToggle = { onToggleSession(session.sessionId) }
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        item { Spacer(Modifier.height(32.dp)) }
    } // end LazyColumn

    // ── Help modal (поверх контента) ────────────────────────────────────
    helpText?.let { text ->
        HelpModal(text = text, onClose = { helpText = null })
    }
    } // end Box
}

        )

        SummaryRow("Days",     data.daysSinceStart.toString(), textColor)
        SummaryRow("Sessions", data.sessionsCount.toString(),  textColor)
        SummaryRow("Play time", formatDuration(data.totalPlayTimeMs), textColor)

        )

}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Period Pill
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun PeriodPill(selected: PeriodFilter, onSelect: (PeriodFilter) -> Unit) {
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
    val accent    = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.accentColor

    Row(
        modifier              = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
            .background(cardBg)
            .padding(4.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(0.dp)
    ) {
        PeriodFilter.entries.forEach { period ->
            val isSelected = period == selected
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(1f)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                    .background(if (isSelected) accent.copy(alpha = 0.18f) else Color.Transparent)
                    .clickable { onSelect(period) }
                    .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                BasicText(
                    text  = period.label,
                    style = labelStyle(
                        size   = 11.sp,
                        weight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.SemiBold else FontWeight.Normal,
                        color  = if (isSelected) accent else textColor.copy(alpha = subtleText)
                    )
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Activation Spectrum (Deviation Profile)
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun ActivationSpectrumCard(data: AnalyticsData, onHelp: (() -> Unit)? = null) {
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor

    StatCard(title = "ACTIVATION SPECTRUM", onHelp = onHelp) {
        // Header
        Row(Modifier.fillMaxWidth()) {
            Spacer(Modifier.width(76.dp))
            BasicText(
                text     = "Median",
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                style    = labelStyle(size = 10.sp,
                    color = textColor.copy(alpha = subtleText),
                    weight = FontWeight.Medium).copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
            )
            BasicText(
                text     = "σ%",
                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                style    = labelStyle(size = 10.sp,
                    color = textColor.copy(alpha = subtleText),
                    weight = FontWeight.Medium).copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
            )
        }

        Spacer(Modifier.height(6.dp))

        SpectrumRow(
            label      = "All",
            subtitle   = "combined",
            medianStr  = formatMs(data.deviationAll.medianMs),
            sigmaStr   = fmtSigma(data.deviationAll.stdPct),
            labelColor = textColor
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
        SpectrumRow(
            label      = "Early",
            subtitle   = "↑ over-excitation",
            medianStr  = formatMs(-data.deviationEarly.medianMs),   // early = abs stored, show negative
            sigmaStr   = fmtSigma(data.deviationEarly.stdPct),
            labelColor = resultRedColor
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
        SpectrumRow(
            label      = "Late",
            subtitle   = "↓ inhibition",
            medianStr  = formatMs(data.deviationLate.medianMs),
            sigmaStr   = fmtSigma(data.deviationLate.stdPct),
            labelColor = resultBlueColor
        )
    }
}

@Composable

    label      : String,
    subtitle   : String,

) {

        Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically

            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            style    = metricStyle(12.sp, FontWeight.Medium, labelColor)
                .copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
        )
        BasicText(
            text     = sigmaStr,
            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
            style    = metricStyle(12.sp, color = labelColor.copy(alpha = 0.75f))
                .copy(textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
        )
    }
}

                            text  = "  ×${dev.count}",

                        )
                    }
                }
                if (data.topEarly.isEmpty()) BasicText("—", style = metricStyle(12.sp))
            }
            // Late column
            Column(Modifier.weight(1f), verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)) {
                BasicText(
                    text  = "Late",
                    style = labelStyle(10.sp, FontWeight.SemiBold, resultBlueColor)

                data.topLate.forEach { dev ->
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        BasicText(
                            text  = formatMs(dev.errorMs),
                            style = metricStyle(12.sp, color = resultBlueColor)
                        )
                        BasicText(
                            text  = "  ×${dev.count}",
                            style = labelStyle(10.sp, color = resultBlueColor.copy(alpha = 0.65f))
                        )
                    }
                }
                if (data.topLate.isEmpty()) BasicText("—", style = metricStyle(12.sp))
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun CardMetricRow(
    label      : String,
    value      : String,
    valueColor : Color = Color.Unspecified,
    note       : String? = null
) {
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
    Row(
        modifier              = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
        verticalAlignment     = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Column {
            BasicText(
                text  = label,
                style = labelStyle(13.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.65f))
            )
            if (note != null) BasicText(
                text  = note,
                style = labelStyle(9.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = subtleText))
            )
        }
        BasicText(
            text  = value,
            style = metricStyle(14.sp, FontWeight.SemiBold,
                if (valueColor == Color.Unspecified) textColor else valueColor)
        )
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Дерево сессий
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun TreeYearRow(year: Int, attempts: Int, wins: Int, expanded: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) =
    TreeRow(0.dp, year.toString(), attempts, wins, expanded,
        metricStyle(14.sp, FontWeight.Bold, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor), onClick)

@Composable
private fun TreeMonthRow(label: String, attempts: Int, wins: Int, expanded: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) =
    TreeRow(12.dp, label, attempts, wins, expanded,
        metricStyle(13.sp, FontWeight.Medium, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor.copy(alpha = 0.85f)), onClick)

@Composable
private fun TreeDayRow(label: String, attempts: Int, wins: Int, expanded: Boolean, onClick: () -> Unit) =
    TreeRow(24.dp, label, attempts, wins, expanded,
        labelStyle(12.sp, color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor.copy(alpha = 0.70f)), onClick)

@Composable
private fun TreeRow(
    indent     : Dp,
    label      : String,
    attempts   : Int,
    wins       : Int,
    expanded   : Boolean,
    labelStyle2: TextStyle,
    onClick    : () -> Unit
) {
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(start = indent, top = 4.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
        verticalAlignment     = Alignment.CenterVertically,
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
    ) {
        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
            BasicText(text = if (expanded) "▾ " else "▸ ", style = labelStyle2.copy(color = labelStyle2.color.copy(alpha = 0.6f)))
            BasicText(text = label, style = labelStyle2)
        }
        BasicText(
            text  = "$attempts / $wins",
            style = metricStyle(11.sp, FontWeight.Normal, textColor.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun TreeSessionRow(
    session  : SessionTreeData,
    expanded : Boolean,
    onToggle : () -> Unit
) {
    val textColor  = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
    val sdf        = remember { SimpleDateFormat("HH:mm", Locale.getDefault()) }
    val startStr   = sdf.format(Date(session.startTime))
    val durationMs = session.endTime - session.startTime
    val winPct     = if (session.attempts > 0)
        (session.wins.toDouble() / session.attempts * 100).roundToInt() else 0

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .clickable { onToggle() }
            .padding(start = 36.dp, top = 4.dp, bottom = 4.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(2.dp)
    ) {
        // ── Header row ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        Row(
            modifier              = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment     = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                BasicText(
                    text  = if (expanded) "▾ " else "▸ ",
                    style = labelStyle(9.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.30f))
                )
                BasicText(
                    text  = startStr,
                    style = metricStyle(11.sp, FontWeight.Normal, textColor.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                )
            }
            BasicText(
                text  = formatDuration(durationMs),
                style = metricStyle(11.sp, FontWeight.Normal, textColor.copy(alpha = 0.40f))
            )
            BasicText(
                text  = "${session.attempts} att",
                style = labelStyle(11.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
            )
            BasicText(
                text  = "$winPct% ✓",
                style = metricStyle(11.sp, FontWeight.Medium, resultGreenColor.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
            )
        }

        // ── Quest text ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (session.questLevelId > 0 || session.questTaskId > 0) {
            BasicText(
                text  = "LVL ${session.questLevelId}  •  task ${session.questTaskId}",
                style = labelStyle(9.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.35f))
            )
        }

        // ── Crown marker ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
        if (session.isQuestComplete) {
            BasicText(
                text  = "👑 Done! Level up",
                style = labelStyle(10.sp, FontWeight.SemiBold, Color(0xFFFFD700))
            )
        }

        // ── Attempt bars + mini histogram (expanded only) ────────────────
        if (expanded && session.attemptBars.isNotEmpty()) {
            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
            AttemptBarsSection(session.attemptBars)
            Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))
            MiniHistogram(session.attemptBars)
            Spacer(Modifier.height(4.dp))
        }
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Attempt Bars (развёрнутая сессия)
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun AttemptBarsSection(bars: List<ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AttemptBarData>) {
    val maxAbs = bars.maxOf { abs(it.errorMs) }.coerceAtLeast(1.0)
    Column(verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(3.dp)) {
        bars.forEach { bar -> AttemptBarRow(bar = bar, maxAbsMs = maxAbs) }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun AttemptBarRow(
    bar      : ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AttemptBarData,
    maxAbsMs : Double
) {
    val color = when {
        bar.isWin       -> resultGreenColor
        bar.errorMs < 0 -> resultRedColor
        else            -> resultBlueColor
    }
    val fraction  = (abs(bar.errorMs) / maxAbsMs).toFloat().coerceIn(0.02f, 1f)
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor

    Row(
        modifier          = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(end = 4.dp),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // номер попытки
        BasicText(
            text     = "%2d".format(bar.number),
            modifier = Modifier.width(20.dp),
            style    = labelStyle(8.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.30f))
        )
        // трек + заливка
        Box(Modifier.weight(1f).height(5.dp)) {
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(dividerClr)
            )
            Box(
                Modifier
                    .fillMaxHeight()
                    .fillMaxWidth(fraction)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp))
                    .background(color.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
            )
        }
        Spacer(Modifier.width(6.dp))
        // значение ошибки
        BasicText(
            text     = formatMs(bar.errorMs),
            modifier = Modifier.width(46.dp),
            style    = metricStyle(8.sp, FontWeight.Normal, color.copy(alpha = 0.72f))
        )
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Help Modal
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun HelpModal(text: String, onClose: () -> Unit) {
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
    val accent    = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.accentColor

    Box(
        modifier         = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.65f))
            .clickable { onClose() },
        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth(0.88f)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                .background(cardBg)
                .padding(20.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {
            BasicText(
                text  = text,
                style = labelStyle(13.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
            )
            BasicText(
                text     = "OK",
                modifier = Modifier
                    .align(Alignment.End)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                    .clickable { onClose() }
                    .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                style    = labelStyle(12.sp, FontWeight.SemiBold, accent)
            )
        }
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Mini Histogram (распределение внутри сессии)
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@Composable
private fun MiniHistogram(bars: List<ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.entity.AttemptBarData>) {
    if (bars.size < 2) return
    val textColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor

    val errors = bars.map { it.errorMs }
    val minE = errors.min()
    val maxE = errors.max()
    if (maxE - minE < 1.0) return   // все одинаковые — нет смысла

    val bucketCount = 9
    val bucketW = (maxE - minE) / bucketCount
    val counts = IntArray(bucketCount)
    errors.forEach { e ->
        val idx = ((e - minE) / bucketW).toInt().coerceIn(0, bucketCount - 1)
        counts[idx]++
    }
    val maxCount = counts.max().coerceAtLeast(1)

    // Бакет, содержащий 0ms (граница early/late)
    val zeroBucket = if (minE < 0 && maxE > 0)
        ((-minE) / bucketW).toInt().coerceIn(0, bucketCount - 1) else -1

    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().padding(end = 4.dp)) {
        BasicText(
            text  = "distribution",
            style = labelStyle(8.sp, color = textColor.copy(alpha = 0.25f))
        )
        Spacer(Modifier.height(3.dp))
        Canvas(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(30.dp)
        ) {
            val gap  = size.width / bucketCount
            val barW = gap * 0.72f

            counts.forEachIndexed { i, count ->
                if (count == 0) return@forEachIndexed
                val barH = (count.toFloat() / maxCount) * size.height * 0.9f
                val bucketCenter = minE + (i + 0.5) * bucketW
                val color = when {
                    i == zeroBucket    -> resultGreenColor.copy(alpha = 0.70f)
                    bucketCenter < 0.0 -> resultRedColor.copy(alpha = 0.65f)
                    else               -> resultBlueColor.copy(alpha = 0.65f)
                }
                val x = gap * i + (gap - barW) / 2
                drawRect(
                    color   = color,
                    topLeft = Offset(x, size.height - barH),
                    size    = Size(barW, barH)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  TrendSparkline
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Маленький линейный график тренда.
 * [values] — float 0..N, старые → новые.
 * [color]  — цвет линии.
 * Высота фиксирована; ширина = fillMaxWidth * доля.
 */
@Composable
private fun TrendSparkline(values: List<Float>, color: Color) {
    if (values.size < 2) return
    Canvas(
        modifier = Modifier
            .height(28.dp)
            .fillMaxWidth()
    ) {
        val minV = values.min()
        val maxV = values.max()
        val range = (maxV - minV).coerceAtLeast(0.001f)
        val w = size.width
        val h = size.height
        val path = Path()
        values.forEachIndexed { i, v ->
            val x = w * i / (values.size - 1)
            val y = h - (v - minV) / range * h * 0.85f - h * 0.075f   // 7.5% padding top/bottom
            if (i == 0) path.moveTo(x, y) else path.lineTo(x, y)
        }
        drawPath(
            path  = path,
            color = color,
            style = Stroke(width = 1.5.dp.toPx(), cap = StrokeCap.Round)
        )
        // точка «сейчас»
        val lastX = w
        val lastY = h - (values.last() - minV) / range * h * 0.85f - h * 0.075f
        drawCircle(color = color, radius = 3.dp.toPx(), center = Offset(lastX, lastY))
    }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Утилиты форматирования
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** σ% с cap: защита от 688% на шумных данных */
private fun fmtSigma(pct: Double): String = when {
    pct <= 0.0  -> "—"
    pct > 999.0 -> ">999%"
    else        -> "%.0f%%".format(pct)
}

private fun formatMs(ms: Double): String {
    val sign  = if (ms < 0) "−" else "+"
    val absMs = abs(ms)
    return when {
        absMs >= 1000.0 -> "${sign}%.2fs".format(absMs / 1000.0)
        else            -> "${sign}%.0fms".format(absMs)
    }
}

private fun formatDuration(ms: Long): String {
    if (ms <= 0L) return "—"
    val totalSec = ms / 1000
    val h = totalSec / 3600
    val m = (totalSec % 3600) / 60
    val s = totalSec % 60
    return if (h > 0) "%d:%02d:%02d".format(h, m, s) else "%d:%02d".format(m, s)
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Дерево год → месяц → день
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

private data class SessionDayNode(val day: Int, val label: String,
    val sessions: List<SessionTreeData>, val totalAttempts: Int, val totalWins: Int)
private data class SessionMonthNode(val month: Int, val label: String,
    val days: List<SessionDayNode>, val totalAttempts: Int, val totalWins: Int)
private data class SessionYearNode(val year: Int, val months: List<SessionMonthNode>,
    val totalAttempts: Int, val totalWins: Int)

private fun buildTree(sessions: List<SessionTreeData>): List<SessionYearNode> {
    val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
    return sessions
        .groupBy { s -> cal.also { it.timeInMillis = s.startTime }.get(Calendar.YEAR) }
        .map { (year, ySessions) ->
            val months = ySessions
                .groupBy { s -> cal.also { it.timeInMillis = s.startTime }.get(Calendar.MONTH) }
                .map { (month, mSessions) ->
                    val days = mSessions
                        .groupBy { s -> cal.also { it.timeInMillis = s.startTime }.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH) }
                        .map { (day, dSessions) ->
                            val dayLabel = cal.also {
                                it.set(year, month, day)
                            }.getDisplayName(Calendar.DAY_OF_WEEK, Calendar.SHORT, Locale.ENGLISH) +
                                    " %02d".format(day)
                            SessionDayNode(day, dayLabel, dSessions,
                                dSessions.sumOf { it.attempts }, dSessions.sumOf { it.wins })
                        }
                        .sortedByDescending { it.day }
                    val monthLabel = cal.also {
                        it.set(year, month, 1)
                    }.getDisplayName(Calendar.MONTH, Calendar.LONG, Locale.ENGLISH) ?: "Month $month"
                    SessionMonthNode(month, monthLabel, days,
                        days.sumOf { it.totalAttempts }, days.sumOf { it.totalWins })
                }
                .sortedByDescending { it.month }
            SessionYearNode(year, months, months.sumOf { it.totalAttempts }, months.sumOf { it.totalWins })
        }
        .sortedByDescending { it.year }
}

====== C:\PlayOfTime\time_mvp-quest_dev\app\src\main\java\ru\iflex\timemvp\features\stats\ui\StatsViewModel.kt ======
package ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui

import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Environment
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import org.apache.poi.xssf.usermodel.XSSFWorkbook
import ru.iflex.timemvp.R
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.BaseViewModel
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.StatisticExcelFields
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.excelFileName
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.orientationMode
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.portraitMode
import ru.iflex.timemvp.core.utils.PeriodFilter
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.domain.usecase.StatUseCases
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui.StatsContract.Effect
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui.StatsContract.Event
import ru.iflex.timemvp.features.stats.ui.StatsContract.State
import ru.iflex.timemvp.use_case.ContextUseCase
import ru.iflex.timemvp.use_case.RecalculateScmUseCase
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.io.IOException

class StatsViewModel(
    private val context: ContextUseCase,
    private val statUseCases: StatUseCases,
    private val recalculateScm: RecalculateScmUseCase
) : BaseViewModel<Event, State, Effect>() {

    private val exportFile = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Default)

    override fun setInitialState() = State()

    init {
        with(context()) {
            Intent().also { intent ->
                intent.setAction(orientationMode)
                intent.putExtra(orientationMode, portraitMode)
                sendBroadcast(intent)
            }
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            // Одноразовый пересчёт исторических SCM (ms → секунды)
            recalculateScm()
            loadAnalytics(PeriodFilter.ALL_TIME)
        }
    }

    private fun loadAnalytics(period: PeriodFilter) {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            setState { copy(analyticsLoading = true) }
            val analytics = statUseCases.analyticsUseCase(period)
            setState { copy(analytics = analytics, analyticsLoading = false, selectedPeriod = period) }
        }
    }

    override fun handleEvents(event: Event) {
        when (event) {
            is Event.SelectPeriod -> loadAnalytics(event.period)

            is Event.ExportToExel -> {
                viewModelScope.launch { setEvent(Event.AppSpinner.Display) }
                exportFile.launch {
                    val totalDaysCount = statUseCases.totalDaysCount()
                    val filePath =
                        (Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(Environment.DIRECTORY_DOWNLOADS).absolutePath) +
                                "/" + totalDaysCount + excelFileName
                    val data = statUseCases.exportToExcelUseCase()
                    val ok = createExcelFile(filePath, data)
                    setState {
                        copy(
                            displayExportToExcelResultDialog = true,
                            exportToExcelResultDialogText = if (ok)
                                context.context.getString(R.string.success_load)
                            else
                                context.context.getString(R.string.fail_load),
                            displayAppSpinner = false
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            is Event.ToggleYear -> setState {
                val years = if (event.year in expandedYears)
                    expandedYears - event.year else expandedYears + event.year
                copy(expandedYears = years)
            }

            is Event.ToggleMonth -> setState {
                val months = if (event.key in expandedMonths)
                    expandedMonths - event.key else expandedMonths + event.key
                copy(expandedMonths = months)
            }

            is Event.ToggleDay -> setState {
                val days = if (event.key in expandedDays)
                    expandedDays - event.key else expandedDays + event.key
                copy(expandedDays = days)
            }

            is Event.ToggleSession -> setState {
                val sessions = if (event.sessionId in expandedSessions)
                    expandedSessions - event.sessionId else expandedSessions + event.sessionId
                copy(expandedSessions = sessions)
            }

            is Event.Dialog.Display -> setState {
                copy(displayExportToExcelResultDialog = true, exportToExcelResultDialogText = event.text)
            }

            is Event.Dialog.Close -> setState {
                copy(displayExportToExcelResultDialog = false, exportToExcelResultDialogText = "")
            }

            is Event.AppSpinner.Display -> setState { copy(displayAppSpinner = true) }
            is Event.AppSpinner.Close -> setState { copy(displayAppSpinner = false) }
        }
    }

    private fun createExcelFile(filePath: String, dataList: List<ExcelStatisticState>): Boolean {
        val workbook = XSSFWorkbook()
        val sheet = workbook.createSheet("Data Sheet")

        val headerRow = sheet.createRow(0)
        headerRow.createCell(0).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.DATE.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(1).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.TIME.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(2).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.SESSION_NUMBER.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(3).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.ATTEMPT_NUMBER.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(4).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.ATTEMPT_RESULT.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(5).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.ERROR_TIME.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(6).setCellValue(StatisticExcelFields.ACCURACY.columnName)
        headerRow.createCell(7).setCellValue("Hand")

        dataList.forEachIndexed { index, entity ->
            val row = sheet.createRow(index + 1)
            row.createCell(0).setCellValue(entity.date)
            row.createCell(1).setCellValue(entity.time)
            row.createCell(2).setCellValue(entity.sessionNumber.toDouble())
            row.createCell(3).setCellValue(entity.attemptNumber.toDouble())
            row.createCell(4).setCellValue(entity.attemptResult.toDouble())
            row.createCell(5).setCellValue(entity.errorTime.toDouble())
            row.createCell(6).setCellValue(entity.accuracy)
            row.createCell(7).setCellValue(entity.hand)
        }

        return try {
            FileOutputStream(filePath).use { workbook.write(it) }
            true
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            false
        } finally {
            workbook.close()
        }
    }
}

    title: String? = null,
    text: String,
    color: Color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.baseTextColor
) {

        modifier = modifier,
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {

            )

                .border(
                    width = 2.dp,
                    color = color,
                    shape = MaterialTheme.shapes.extraSmall
                )
                .padding(Dimens.smallPadding)
                .fillMaxWidth(),
            text = text,
            style = baseTextStyle.copy(color = color, textAlign = TextAlign.Center)
        )
    }
}
